Neural networks have been successfully employed to model TPPs in a highly expressive and data-driven way. Neural TPPs are typically trained via Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE) by minimizing the negative log-likelihood (NLL), which depends on both the conditional intensity function (CIF) and its integral over time, the compensator. Recent neural TPP approaches enable exact evaluation of the NLL without numerical integration. However, these methods typically model the compensator rather than the CIF directly, impose constraints on the neural network architecture, and are computationally expensive during training, as event contributions to the NLL are evaluated sequentially rather than in parallel. In this work, we propose a novel neural TPP model that directly parametrizes the CIF as a non-negative combination of B-spline basis functions, whose coefficients are predicted by a neural network. This formulation enables exact evaluation of the NLL, preserves full flexibility in the neural architecture, allows efficient parallelization during training, and naturally supports CIF smoothness regularization through the integrated squared second derivative. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show improved computational efficiency and predictive accuracy compared to the reference neural TPP baseline.

</details>


### [213] [TOUR: A Trajectory-Level Unlearning Benchmark for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21111)
*Chaofan Pan,Lingfei Ren,Xiangyu Jiang,Yanhua Li,Xuemei Cao,Xiangkun Wang,Hao Yu,Wei Wei,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TOUR基准框架，用于评估离线强化学习中的轨迹级遗忘效果，发现在D4RL locomotion任务中，基于单一似然度量的删除评估可能高估删除质量，结论依赖于多个因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习（Offline RL）代理在固定行为轨迹上训练，当需要删除选定数据时，轨迹级删除至关重要。然而，评估此类删除效果存在困难：较低的记忆分数可能反映轨迹移除、残留记忆被其他攻击检测到，或策略崩溃导致有用行为丧失。现有评估方法缺乏统一标准。

Method: 提出TOUR（Trajectory-level memOrization and Unlearning in offline RL）基准框架，包含轨迹级分区、匹配的非成员对照、重训练参考、保留性能锚点以及多攻击隐私审计。在D4RL locomotion实验和AntMaze扩展上进行评估。

Result: 常见删除基线在环境依赖下表现出不同的隐私-效用行为；重训练和微调通常比均匀GA+Refit提供更强的保留效用参考；单一基于似然度的成员分数可能高估删除质量；关于离线RL遗忘的结论在单分数审计下不稳定。

Conclusion: 评估离线RL遗忘效果需考虑匹配的非成员构造、重训练相对校准、攻击家族、保留效用以及诊断架构或组件级证据的显式范围，单一审计方法不足以得出稳定结论。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are trained on fixed behavioral trajectories, which makes trajectory-level deletion important when selected data must be removed after training. Evaluating such deletion is difficult because a lower membership score can reflect trajectory removal, residual memorization visible to another attack, or policy collapse that destroys useful behavior. We introduce Trajectory-level memOrization and Unlearning in offline RL (TOUR), a benchmark that combines trajectory-level partitioning, matched non-member controls, retraining references, retained-performance anchors, and multi-attack privacy auditing. Across D4RL locomotion experiments and an exploratory AntMaze extension, TOUR shows that common deletion baselines have environment-dependent privacy-utility behavior. Retraining and fine-tuning often provide stronger retained-utility references than uniform GA+Refit, while TrajDeleter remains a useful comparator but is not uniformly stronger under the same audit. Reference-model, threshold, deviation, equivalence, action-error, representation-based, and query-limited attacks further show that a single likelihood-based membership score can overstate deletion quality. In the evaluated settings, conclusions about offline RL unlearning are therefore not stable under single-score auditing. They depend on matched non-member construction, retraining-relative calibration, attack family, retained utility, and explicit scope for diagnostic architecture or component-level evidence.

</details>


### [214] [GlucoTune: A Unified Framework for Blood Glucose Preprocessing, Forecasting, and Benchmarking in Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21117)
*Davide Marelli,Giorgia Rigamonti,Mirko Paolo Barbato,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoTune 是一个用于1型糖尿病血糖时间序列数据预处理和模型评估的标准化、可复现框架，通过可配置的YAML流水线、统一接口和基准排行榜，解决了数据预处理缺乏标准化、可复现性差和数据共享受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血糖时间序列数据预处理缺乏标准化工作流和评估协议，导致研究可复现性差、跨研究公平比较困难，且隐私和许可限制阻碍了预处理后的医学数据集重新分发。

Method: 构建可扩展框架，通过可移植YAML配置文件定义可配置预处理流水线实现可复现预处理；提供统一接口用于实现、训练和评估血糖预测模型；集成公共数据集标准化包装器；收录最新血糖预测和通用时序预测方法；建立基准排行榜实现跨数据集、预处理配置和预测方法的系统比较。

Result: 通过综合实验证明了GlucoTune的有效性，并通过用户研究评估了其可用性。

Conclusion: GlucoTune为血糖时间序列数据的可复现实验提供了标准化框架，解决了预处理不一致和数据共享限制等关键问题，有助于推动糖尿病管理数据驱动方法的可复现性和公平比较。

Abstract: Preprocessing blood glucose time-series data is a critical yet often overlooked step in developing data-driven methods for diabetes management, particularly for type 1 diabetes. The lack of standardized preprocessing workflows and evaluation protocols hinders reproducibility and complicates fair comparison across studies. These challenges are further exacerbated by data-sharing restrictions, as privacy and licensing constraints often prevent the redistribution of preprocessed medical datasets. To address these limitations, we present GlucoTune, a comprehensive and extensible framework for reproducible experimentation with blood glucose time-series data. The framework standardizes the entire experimental workflow, from preprocessing to model evaluation, enabling reproducible experiments directly from the original datasets. Reproducible preprocessing is achieved through configurable pipelines defined in portable YAML configuration files, ensuring consistent data handling without distributing sensitive preprocessed data. Beyond preprocessing, GlucoTune provides a unified interface for implementing, training, and evaluating blood glucose prediction models. The framework integrates public datasets through standardized wrappers and provides a curated collection of state-of-the-art blood glucose prediction and general time-series forecasting methods, while remaining readily extensible to additional datasets, preprocessing strategies, and forecasting models. To promote transparent and consistent evaluation, GlucoTune includes a benchmarking leaderboard that reports results across datasets, preprocessing configurations, and forecasting methods, enabling systematic comparison of experimental settings. We demonstrate the effectiveness of GlucoTune through comprehensive experiments and assess its usability in a user study.

</details>


### [215] [Relative Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21120)
*Marc Höftmann,Jan Robine,Stefan Harmeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出相对价值学习(RV)框架，通过反对称函数直接学习价值差异，并证明其理论基础。将RV与PPO结合，在Atari基准测试中取得与标准PPO竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习中评论家估计绝对状态价值V(s)，但控制任务实际上只关心价值差异，因此存在根本性的不匹配。

Method: 提出RV框架，定义反对称函数Δ(s_i,s_j)=V(s_i)-V(s_j)直接学习价值差异；引入成对Bellman算子并证明其γ-压缩性；推导1步、n步和λ-return目标；从成对差异重建广义优势估计得到无偏策略梯度估计器R-GAE。

Result: RV与PPO结合，在49个ALE游戏的Atari基准测试中取得与标准PPO竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 相对价值估计是绝对评论家的有效替代方案。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, critics typically estimate absolute state values $V(s)$, estimating how good a particular situation is in isolation. However, it turns out that only differences in value are relevant for control. Motivated by this, we propose Relative Value Learning (RV), a framework that learns value differences directly via an antisymmetric function $Δ(s_i, s_j) = V(s_i) - V(s_j)$. We introduce a pairwise Bellman operator and prove it is a $γ$-contraction with a unique fixed point equal to the true value differences, derive well-posed $1$-step, $n$-step and $λ$-return targets and reconstruct generalized advantage estimation from pairwise differences to obtain an unbiased policy-gradient estimator (R-GAE). Beyond theoretical results, we integrate RV with PPO and achieve competitive performance on the Atari benchmark (49 ALE games) compared to standard PPO, indicating that relative value estimation is an effective alternative to absolute critics.

</details>


### [216] [Demographically-Informed Heat-Mortality Risk Curves via Risk Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21131)
*Alex O. Davies,Eunice Lo,Rui Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs)，一种层级图神经网络编码器，利用细粒度人口普查特征优化DLNM系数向量，在保持可解释风险曲线输出的同时显著提升预测校准能力。在英格兰和威尔士10个地区两个极端高温年份的评估中，RGNN变体在2022年热浪期间保持了较低的点误差和接近标称的不确定性覆盖，而基线方法则失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLNM模型在估计热相关 mortality 风险时忽略了人口统计和地理背景信息，而这些因素已被证实与热脆弱性密切相关。

Method: 提出Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs)，一种层级GNN编码器，利用细粒度人口普查特征来优化DLNM的系数向量，从而在保留可解释风险曲线输出的同时改善预测校准。

Result: 在英格兰和威尔士10个地区两个极端高温年份的评估中，RGNN变体在2022年热浪期间保持较低的点误差和接近标称的不确定性覆盖，而基线方法则失效。

Conclusion: RGNN通过引入人口统计和地理上下文信息，有效提升了热相关 mortality 风险预测的准确性和可靠性，特别是在极端热浪事件中显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Estimating heat-related mortality risk is a core task in environmental epidemiology, typically addressed with Distributed Lag Non-linear Models (DLNMs); interpretable exposure-response surfaces fitted to temperature-mortality time series. DLNMs are effective but ignore demographic and geographic context, despite well-established relevance to heat vulnerability. We propose Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs), a hierarchical GNN encoder that uses granular census features to optimise DLNM coefficient vectors, preserving interpretable risk curve outputs while substantially improving predictive calibration. Evaluated across 10 regions of England and Wales on two unprecedented heat years, RGNN variants maintain both lower point-errors and near-nominal uncertainty coverage during the 2022 heatwave where baselines collapse.

</details>


### [217] [Agree on the Model, Verify the Inference: GKR Protocols for HND-Based Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21162)
*Xiaolong Liang,Juanjuan Li,Rui Qin,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GKR-HND协议，通过注册模型和同态-非同态分解验证Transformer推理的正确性，将繁重的公开计算委托给计算工人，避免完全重放的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包Transformer推理面临模型替换和不完全执行的威胁，而直接重放验证会丧失委托计算的计算优势，需要在轻量验证与安全保证之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用注册模型协议，验证同态-非同态分解Transformer的多项式骨干；保留验证者检查GKR转录和注册权重公开值，将公开计算委托给计算工人；依赖保留验证者的诚实性和证明者-工人非共谋假设，通过工人的签名响应与证明声明一致来接受。

Result: 在预训练HND模型上的实验验证了证明路径和委托公开计算的有效性，无需密集矩阵重放。

Conclusion: GKR-HND在保证安全性的前提下，通过选择性委托避免了完全重放的计算开销，实现了高效且可验证的外包Transformer推理。

Abstract: Outsourced Transformer inference exposes clients to model substitution and incomplete execution, while direct replay removes the computational benefit of delegation. We present GKR-HND, a registered-model protocol for verifying the polynomial backbone of Homomorphic--Nonhomomorphic Decomposition Transformers. The retained verifier checks the GKR transcript and registered-weight openings, but delegates expensive public evaluations to an assigned computation worker. Assuming an honest retained verifier and prover--worker non-collusion, the verifier accepts only when the worker's signed, request-bound response agrees with the proof claims. Experiments with pretrained HND models validate the proof path and the delegated public computation without dense-matrix replay.

</details>


### [218] [Automated Synthesis and Adversarial Validation of Executable Causal Research Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21173)
*Irena Girshovitz,Dan Zeltzer,Ran Gilad-Bachrach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARA框架通过显式编码因果设计原则和对抗性验证，将自动科研系统的因果推断从"静默失败"转变为"可质疑的失败"——即使不总能提升数值准确度，但能主动暴露因果假设的无效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科研系统在执行分析代码时可能产生"静默失败"（代码成功运行但依赖无效因果假设），现有方法无法使这些失败可见，迫切需要一种能显式暴露因果假设问题的框架。

Method: ARA框架将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议，基于结构因果模型生成已知真实效应的合成数据（支持受限数据场景下的管道开发），并在受控违反识别假设的条件下进行对抗性验证，评估分析结果的可靠性。

Result: 与标准LLM生成相比，协议构建和对抗性验证并未持续改善与基准估计的数值一致性，但改变了失败模式：ARA更常报告协议问题、诊断失败、不完整推断或降级为非因果解释，而非静默返回因果估计。

Conclusion: 因果优先的自动科学系统不仅应通过答案准确性评估，还应通过系统能否在因果主张不可靠时主动发出信号来评估。

Abstract: While automated research systems promise to accelerate empirical analysis, they are prone to silent failures: instances in which analysis code executes successfully yet relies on invalid causal assumptions. We present the Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Epidemiology Research Assistant (ARA), a framework that makes these failures visible by explicitly encoding causal design principles, study-specific assumptions, and methodological constraints. ARA integrates protocol construction, synthetic data generation, and adversarial validation into a unified pipeline. The framework translates natural language research questions into structured causal protocols and executable analysis code by first constructing a protocol and then generating synthetic datasets using Structural Causal Models (SCMs) with known ground-truth effects. This synthetic-data step can also support pipeline development when access to confidential data, such as medical data, is restricted. The generated analysis is then evaluated under controlled violations of identification assumptions. We evaluate ARA on the Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark, assessing recovery of identification strategies, causal quantities, treatment and outcome variables, and consistency between generated code and approved protocol. Protocol construction and adversarial validation did not consistently improve numerical agreement with benchmark estimates compared with standard LLM-based generation. However, they changed the failure mode: instead of silently returning causal estimates, ARA often surfaced protocol concerns, diagnostic failures, incomplete inference, or downgraded non-causal interpretations. These findings suggest that validity-first automated science systems should be evaluated not only by answer accuracy, but also by whether they indicate when causal claims are unwarranted.

</details>


### [219] [Filter Learning for Subgraphs: Algebras and Performance Risk Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21263)
*Purui Zhang,Feng Ji,Yanan Zhao,Bihan Wen,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出子图滤波器学习（SFL）框架，利用距离感知拉普拉斯算子构造子图滤波器代数，在部分图观测下近似全局图滤波器，并通过理论界和实验验证其优于多项式滤波器等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图信号处理中的谱方法通常假设完整图拓扑可用，但实际中常只能观测到部分图结构（子图）。如何在子图支持下有效近似全局图滤波器，是一个未充分解决的统计学习问题。

Method: 将SFL形式化为统计学习问题，构建基于距离感知拉普拉斯算子的子图滤波器代数，定义结构化的可控制滤波器类；建立最小二乘损失下的性能风险界，量化子图算子对受限全局映射的逼近程度。

Result: 在真实数据集上，所提出的代数模型在SFL任务中一致优于多项式滤波器、分布无关算子和直接数值滤波器学习基线。

Conclusion: 子图滤波器代数提供了一种有效且可控的局部观测下全局谱滤波近似方案，理论与实验共同验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Graph signal processing tasks that leverage spectral information typically assume access to the complete graph topology, which is often unavailable in practice. We propose a systematic framework for subgraph filter learning (SFL), where subgraph-supported operators approximate ambient graph filters under partial observations. We formulate SFL as a statistical learning problem in which optimal subgraph operators are inherently data-dependent. To address the difficulty of directly estimating such operators, we develop a subgraph filter algebra based on distance-aware Laplacian constructions, defining a structured and controllable class of filters for effective approximation. We further establish performance risk bounds under the least squares loss, quantifying how well the learned operator approximates the restricted ambient mapping. Experiments real-world datasets show that, for SFL tasks, the proposed algebraic models consistently outperform polynomial filters, distribution-agnostic operators, and direct numerical filter learning baselines that attempt to recover the underlying structure from data.

</details>


### [220] [The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现在GRPO（组归一化强化学习）下，基于预测的稠密奖励不仅无效，反而会摧毁策略，导致"黑暗房间"病态（预测准确率→1.0，任务成功率→0）。单因素消融定位到GRPO的std归一化是罪魁祸首，提出z-score放大机制的理论解释，并通过控制实验证明奖励通道最多中性，而辅助损失通道可提升~20点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长 horizon、稀疏奖励的LLM智能体，稠密的每步监督（预测下一观测作为奖励）是一种有吸引力的补救方案。本文旨在检验该方案在组归一化RL（GRPO）下的实际效果。

Method: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上结合ALFWorld环境进行实验，使用基于势能的预测奖励；通过单因素消融（移除GRPO的std归一化）定位原因；提出z-score放大机制的理论分析；构建控制信号递送矩阵（相同信号，不同消费机制）进行对比实验。

Result: 稠密预测奖励在GRPO下导致策略崩溃（预测准确率趋近1，任务成功率归零，回合长度固定在horizon）。移除std归一化后，相同奖励从灾难性（0%）恢复为基线水平。奖励通道表现中性，辅助损失通道提升约20点，且随机打乱的金标准（shuffled-gold）与真实金标准效果相当。

Conclusion: GRPO的z-score归一化会放大稠密信号在组内（尤其全失败组）的方差，使有界奖励变为无界压力，这是导致策略崩溃的根本机制。方差衰减特性是判断信号是否结构安全的关键准则。

Abstract: Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [221] [Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Prediction from Video Game State with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21290)
*Jonas Peché,Aliaksei Tsishurou,Alexander Zap,Günter Wallner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在团队多人游戏中，使用多任务学习（MTL）联合训练共享模型能否提升泛化能力，同时降低训练和推理成本。提出一种适配多任务设置的多模态架构，结合栅格化视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和逐单位状态信息，通过图像编码器和注意力机制进行交互建模。在大型《坦克世界》数据集上实验，对比单任务与多任务训练、评估混合损失加权策略和冲突梯度处理，并测试有限目标数据下的预训练/微调，以及游戏地图间的结构化环境迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用现代游戏遥测技术提供的多个相关监督信号，探索多任务学习能否在团队多人游戏中对结构化状态数据实现更好的泛化，同时减少训练和推理成本。

Method: 提出一种通用多任务架构，融合栅格化视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和逐单位状态信息，使用图像编码器和基于注意力的交互建模进行多任务联合训练。

Result: 在《坦克世界》数据集上，对比了单任务与多任务训练效果，评估了混合损失加权策略和冲突梯度处理方法，并验证了有限目标数据下的预训练/微调性能及跨地图迁移效果。

Conclusion: 多任务学习在团队多人游戏预测任务中具有提升泛化能力、降低训练和推理成本的潜力，但仍需仔细设计损失加权和梯度冲突处理策略。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is a promising approach for prediction tasks derived from video game state data, as modern game telemetry provides multiple related supervision signals from the same structured observations. We study whether a shared model trained jointly across tasks in team-based multiplayer games can improve generalization while reducing training and inference cost compared to specialized single-task models. We adapt a multimodal architecture for endpoint prediction to a general multi-task setting that combines rasterized vision inputs, global match context, and per-unit state information through an image encoder and attention-based interaction modeling. Experiments on a large proprietary World of Tanks dataset compare single-task and multi-task training, evaluate weighting strategies for mixed losses and conflicting gradients, and test pre-training/fine-tuning under limited target-data regimes. We also examine within-game transfer across game maps under structured environment shift.

</details>


### [222] [AI Assistants Overassist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21306)
*Verona Teo,Raghav Jain,Tobias Gerstenberg,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Int-Bench——一个基于模拟的基准测试，用于评估LLM在学习过程中何时、是否以及如何介入干预。研究发现，相比人类，LLM教师介入更频繁、更早，且倾向于直接提供完整解决方案而非提示，这优化了短期成功却可能损害深度学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手在问题解决中的干预决策（何时、如何介入）对学习效果至关重要，但当前LLM系统的干预行为尚缺乏系统理解和评估。

Method: 设计Int-Bench基准，模拟"学生"解决问题、"教师"LLM监控推理过程并决定是否干预。涵盖代码调试、数学、脑筋急转弯三个领域，比较LLM与人类在干预频率、时机及对任务成功和泛化能力的影响。

Result: LLM比人类介入更频繁、更早；倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示；虽能提升短期任务成功率，但可能不利于长期学习和泛化。

Conclusion: 当前LLM助手的干预策略优化短期成功，而非支持深层学习所需的推理过程，这与人类教学行为存在显著差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as tutors and thought partners, helping users reason through problems. While guidance from AI assistants can scaffold thinking and foster learning, such benefits depend on how they help--for instance, intervening too early or too frequently may hinder true learning and cognitive engagement. Yet how AI systems navigate intervention decisions during problem-solving remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Int-Bench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating LLM interventions during learning. Int-Bench simulates a "student" solving a problem while a "teacher" monitors the student's reasoning and decides whether, when, and how to intervene. Across three domains--code debugging, mathematics, and brain teasers--we evaluate LLM teachers on the frequency and timing of interventions, as well as their impact on both immediate task success and generalization to new problems. We also compare LLMs to humans, finding that LLMs intervene more frequently and earlier than humans. Moreover, in contrast to humans, they tend to provide complete solutions rather than targeted hints. These findings suggest that current LLM assistants often optimize for short-term success rather than supporting the reasoning processes needed for deeper learning and long-term success.

</details>


### [223] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M³-Gen框架，通过条件生成对抗网络结合组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，利用对比学习构建统一潜在表示，并在TCGA数据集上验证了生成数据的真实性和生物学意义，同时通过注意力机制提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据获取受高成本和隐私问题限制，阻碍了其在多模态研究和AI驱动应用中的使用，因此需要能够从其他模态（如组织病理学图像和临床元数据）生成基因表达谱的方法。

Method: 提出M³-Gen框架，使用条件生成对抗网络（GAN），通过对比学习从临床变量和组织病理学图像中学习统一潜在表示，并利用两种模态的嵌入引导生成模型产生生物学一致的基因表达谱；同时采用基于注意力机制的多模态融合实现内在可解释性。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上，M³-Gen能够生成真实且功能有意义的基因表达数据；注意力机制可识别组织病理学图像中哪些区域对特定基因表达谱的生成影响最大。

Conclusion: M³-Gen成功实现了从组织病理学图像和临床元数据到基因表达谱的跨模态生成，为多模态生物医学数据分析提供了新范式，且模型具有内在可解释性。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [224] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信息论方法测量低秩适配器（LoRA）在冻结基座模型上实际写入的比特数，发现适配器存储的信息量远低于全参数微调，且其容量更多取决于参数所在位置（如MLP层比注意力层能存储近两倍信息）而非参数数量，同时在隐私泄露方面，基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练比监督微调更安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"LoRA适配器是技能而非数据记录"这一普遍假设，通过量化分析揭示适配器实际存储的信息量及其影响因素。

Method: 将压缩式记忆分析扩展到冻结基座模型设置，直接以比特为单位测量低秩适配器在未更改的模型中写入了多少信息。

Result: 适配器每个可训练参数存储约几个比特，远低于完整模型的预算；参数放在MLP层比注意力层容量高出近一倍；去掉冻结基座的结构后容量几乎消失；隐私泄露与适配器写入的比特数相关而非参数数量；监督微调会逐字复制秘密，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练则不会记录。

Conclusion: 将微调写入信息的测量从事后攻击转变为可量化的设计指标，使研究者能够针对性地设计更安全的微调方法。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [225] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验发现，SalUn等基于显著性的权重选择机制在表示级遗忘中并未发挥实质性作用，遗忘效果主要由梯度集中在最后几层、表示几何特性等结构因素主导。研究建议直接作用于潜在表示的目标函数，而非复杂的权重选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于梯度显著性的权重选择方法被广泛应用于机器遗忘，但其对表示级遗忘的实际贡献尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一理解空白，通过控制变量的实验设计，系统评估显著性掩码机制在表示级遗忘中的真正作用。

Method: 采用匹配计算量的实验设计，在CIFAR-10/100和ResNet-18上，对比显著性掩码、随机掩码（等稀疏度）和无约束更新三种策略，保持遗忘目标、优化计划和计算预算不变。使用线性探测、原型恢复和层间CKA等多种表示级评估指标。

Result: 三种掩码策略在表示级可恢复性上统计等价；梯度能量约92%集中在最后几层；显著性掩码的类别特异性很低（特异性指数0.09-0.11），不同遗忘类别选择的参数子集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 表示级遗忘主要由梯度集中和表示几何决定，而非显著性选择的特定权重；有效的表示级遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表示的目标函数，而非更复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [226] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在安全对齐模型上进行窄域不良微调会导致模型在无关问题上出现广泛失调（涌现性失调）。研究发现，这种窄域学习会调用微调前已存在的**人格子空间结构**（persona subspace）。从冻结的Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct中提取的多个无关领域共享一个低秩核心（信噪比657倍于随机子空间），其中82%位于风格核心之外。不良微调的第一步优化即比教育性框架更剧烈地爬升失调边际，并预测后续步数的边际变化。在残差流中投影去除该子空间可完全防止广泛失调（27.7%→0.0%），而注入该子空间到未微调模型可诱导高达45.4%的失调。该结构具有鲁棒性：权重梯度投影无效，三种事后编辑无法根除（最剧烈的编辑仅抑制而非移除，且切除的结构会在编辑清除的子空间内重新形成）。将固定预算的不良数据分散到4个领域比权重叠加和匹配多样性共同解释的失调更严重。所有测量基于14B模型；提取自对齐指令微调检查点，结构起源待定；防止失调的干预也消除了窄域训练行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么对语言模型在窄域不良数据上进行微调会导致在无关问题上出现广泛失调（涌现性失调），以及这种泛化的内在机制。

Method: 从冻结的Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct中通过对比教师强制提取每个域的人格子空间；分析子空间的低秩核心、风格核心分离性；追踪优化第一步的失调边际变化；通过子空间投影/注入、权重梯度投影、事后权重编辑等多种干预手段验证结构因果性；使用固定不良数据预算跨域分散实验排除机械解释。

Result: 多个无关领域共享一个低秩核心（信噪比657倍）；82%核心位于风格核心外；第一步优化即显现失调差异；残差流投影可完全防止广泛失调（27.7%→0.0%）；子空间注入可诱导失调达45.4%；权重梯度投影无效；事后编辑无法根除（结构会重新形成）；跨域分散数据产生超出机械叠加和匹配多样性的失调。

Conclusion: 窄域不良微调引发广泛失调的原因是调用了模型中预存的人格子空间结构，该结构具有低秩、跨域共享、部分独立于风格、优化早期即起作用、对多种干预鲁棒等特性。通过残差流投影可以有效预防这种失调，但会牺牲窄域训练效果。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [227] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOPE是一个将网络压缩从离散域转移到希尔伯特连续函数空间的数学框架。通过将单个神经元建模为秩-1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子，它将剪枝和神经元合并统一为低秩子空间投影，并引入宏块驱逐以统一处理多层结构。该框架无需数据和超参数，通过模型压缩和微调实验验证了其实用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂表征，但解构这些内部知识仍具挑战性。网络压缩虽有潜力，但标准压缩方法常受尺度对称性和架构偏差影响，需要一种无偏的数学框架来解构训练后网络权重的表征。

Method: 将网络压缩从离散域转移到希尔伯特连续函数空间；将神经元建模为秩-1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子，统一剪枝和神经元合并为低秩子空间投影；引入宏块驱逐以统一度量处理多层结构（如完整残差路径）；框架无需数据和超参数。

Result: 通过模型压缩和微调的概念验证实验，展示了该理论框架的实践潜力。

Conclusion: HOPE提供了一种无偏、统一、无需数据和超参数的数学框架，能够渐进解构训练后网络权重的表征，并具有实用的压缩和微调潜力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [228] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD)并发现其得分比率可能违反贝叶斯可实现性，导致负采样权重。为解决此问题，提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法，通过预测干净标记后验均值并利用核相关线性映射转换为得分，确保投影到桥多面体上。M2S在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText上显著优于现有离散扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDD的参数化仅保证正向跳转率非负，但不保证贝叶斯可实现性（即噪声状态下的得分比率不能由任何干净标记后验在正向核下联合导出），导致训练好的模型中大量得分向量违反坐标盒约束或与有效后验不兼容，进而产生负预归一化权重，影响采样质量。

Method: 提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法：先预测干净标记的后验均值，再通过精确的核相关线性映射将其转换为得分。该构造适用于任何满足温和支持条件的已知坐标连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。对于均匀损坏，M2S将概率单纯形映射到桥多面体上；对于吸收掩码损坏，所得目标恰好恢复MD4。

Result: 将原始得分投影到桥多面体上可消除所有观察到的负权重，并将外部生成PPL从203.6降至175.1（不改变采样器）。在受控的28.4M参数CIFAR-10对比中，M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著提升。170M参数M2S模型在约262B OpenWebText token上训练后，在每一个测试采样预算下均优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC，128步时生成PPL达143.3 vs 纯均匀基线183.6。

Conclusion: M2S通过显式施加贝叶斯可实现性约束，有效解决了SEDD中得分比率不兼容的问题，提升了离散扩散模型的生成质量和采样效率。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [229] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种条件时间序列扩散模型方法，用于构建"JITAI-Twins"数字孪生体，在JITAI部署前模拟目标子群体，以评估在线算法设计。该方法通过三步更新（预训练、微调、推理时校准）确保时间一致性，并在HeartSteps系列干预实验中得到验证，能更好复现目标群体的时间与个体间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中算法设计不佳可能导致参与者负担增加和参与度下降，因此在实际部署前需要用逼真的模拟用户对新算法进行充分评估，但目前缺乏有效的预部署模拟方法。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型构建数字孪生体，确保时间一致性（未来动作不影响已生成的过去）。通过三步信息更新：①在大规模观测数据上预训练；②在相关群体的先前的干预部署数据上微调；③利用领域专家知识在推理时校准到目标群体。

Result: 在HeartSteps系列实验（v2至v4）的预部署阶段验证，该方法相比简单模拟器能更好地复现目标子群体的时间序列结构和参与者间差异结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的数字孪生体可在实际部署前有效模拟目标干预环境，为测试和指导在线算法设计决策提供前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [230] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对合作式多任务语义通信中的建设性与破坏性合作问题，提出了一种基于语义感知的任务聚类方法。该方法在初始训练后进行聚类，然后仅在聚类组内进行端到端联合训练。仿真表明该方法能有效缓解负迁移，提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式多任务语义通信中，多任务合作可能是建设性的也可能是破坏性的，取决于任务间的语义关系。现有方法未区分这两种情况，导致负迁移问题。

Method: 提出语义感知的任务聚类方法：先进行短时初始训练，然后利用层次化基于密度的空间聚类（HDBSCAN）将语义对齐的任务聚类，之后仅在聚类组内进行端到端联合训练。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提框架有效缓解了破坏性合作和负迁移，相比未聚类的多任务学习和单独训练基线均获得了准确率提升。

Conclusion: 语义感知的任务聚类能够确保合作式多任务语义通信的建设性合作，避免破坏性影响。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [231] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文加权的离散流匹配方法，通过修改连续时间马尔可夫链和加权交叉熵损失，利用局部上下文信息提升离散生成模型的采样质量和训练效率，在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低高达63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因子化训练目标将不同难度的Token混合训练（条件良好的可预测Token与高熵模糊Token），导致模型训练效率低下。作者观察到每个Token的不确定性与邻域可用上下文的密度密切相关，因此希望利用局部上下文信息来改进离散生成模型。

Method: 1) 提出上下文加权采样器（context-weighted sampler），通过修改底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）来融入局部上下文信息，几乎不增加计算开销；2) 提出缩放交叉熵损失函数（scaled cross-entropy loss），对不同Token的训练信号重新加权；3) 在保持任意顺序生成能力的同时，匹配强半自回归块扩散基线质量。

Result: 在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低最多63%；以极小计算开销显著提升生成质量；在质量上与强半自回归块扩散基线持平，同时保留任意顺序生成能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的重要因素，简单的上下文感知改进就能显著提升采样和训练效率，为离散流匹配框架提供了一种高效且实用的增强方案。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [232] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant提出了一种基于Kronecker结构的可逆变换方法，用于扩散变换器( Diffusion Transformers)的W4A4低位后训练量化(PTQ)。该方法对每个32元素激活块应用学习到的Kronecker结构化可逆变换，参数少于逐通道缩放方法，推理速度比SmoothQuant快14%，在PixArt-Σ、SANA和FLUX.1-schnell上达到比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更优的量化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器(DiTs)在W4A4后训练量化中，由于激活异常值使4位格式无法精确表示，导致输出质量严重下降。现有方法如SmoothQuant计算廉价但量化精度受损；固定Hadamard变换需要大块大小导致高昂在线成本；学习全维度可逆变换校准最优但需要每层每步执行密集的dxd矩阵乘法，计算成本过高。因此需要一种在量化精度与推理计算代价之间取得更好平衡的方法。

Method: KroQuant方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 对每32元素激活块应用学习到的Kronecker结构化可逆变换（块局部结构以小张量核心GEMM运行），参数少于逐通道缩放的一半；(2) 离线LoRaQ权重校准以吸收残差逐权重量化误差。该方法在MI350 GPU上比SmoothQuant量化器内核快14%。

Result: 在PixArt-Σ、SANA和FLUX.1-schnell上以W4A4（MXFP4e2）配置测试，KroQuant在MJHQ-30K和SDCI数据集上生成的结果比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，同时保持或改善了图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构化可逆变换和离线LoRaQ权重校准，在扩散变换器W4A4量化中实现了比现有方法更好的精度-效率权衡，参数高效且推理加速。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [233] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTEL是一种利用环境反馈进行token级错误定位的推理时缩放算法，通过隔离错误步骤并重用有效前缀来提升计算效率，在编程和数学推理任务上建立了严格占优的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时计算方法（如独立采样、多轮精炼）缺乏token级别的信用分配，导致有效推理前缀频繁被丢弃，计算效率低下。

Method: TTEL利用固定或环境反馈进行token级错误定位，通过比较在知情反馈下的条件概率与无上下文基线，隔离出发生错误的步骤，然后截断轨迹并分支生成新内容，最大化重用有效前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，使用Qwen3-8B，TTEL的pass@64达到71.0%，生成token数仅为独立采样的一半（360.4k vs 735.0k）；在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均优于其他推理时基线方法。

Conclusion: TTEL在序列推理领域建立了严格占优的Pareto前沿（以pass-at-k与生成token成本衡量），验证了token级错误定位可显著提升推理时计算效率。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [234] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性KV缓存驱逐（保留top-k重要token）无法知道丢弃内容，因为被驱逐的值可以被篡改而保持保留内容不变但注意力输出误差任意增大。采用泊松采样尾部与Hájek校正的随机化驱逐可恢复可识别性，并实现97%经验覆盖率的逐步骤误差证书。在真实工作负载上，问题感知驱逐在25-50%预算下几乎免费；输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败；证书门控的预算提升无效。留存结论：证书可分离缓存导致与固有的失败（AUC 0.73-0.75 vs 输出置信度0.47-0.54），并能比随机或置信度门控更好地调度重计算。随机化买来的是归因，而非预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性KV缓存驱逐无法知道被丢弃内容的影响，导致注意力输出误差可能任意增大且无法被服务时误差估计器一致估计，因此需要新的理论和方法解决这一不可识别性问题。

Method: 提出随机化驱逐方法：使用泊松采样尾部（已知包含概率），通过一个logit偏移在softmax内执行Hájek校正，并利用调查抽样方差估计器对保留集构建逐步骤误差证书。在真实工作负载上预注册七个假设进行验证。

Result: 1) 问题感知驱逐在25-50%预算下几乎免费；2) 输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败；3) 证书门控的预算提升无效；4) 证书可有效分离缓存导致与固有的失败（AUC 0.73-0.75 vs 输出置信度0.47-0.54）；5) 证书比随机或置信度门控更好地调度重计算。

Conclusion: 确定性驱逐存在不可识别性缺陷，随机化驱逐虽不能完美预测失败，但能为归因提供理论基础（即区分缓存导致与固有的失败），随机化买来的是归因能力而非预测能力。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [235] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在经验流程中，如何通过有限样本有效认证第一轮高召回阶段遗漏的相关项数量。核心发现是：仅对候选集内部标注无法认证遗漏量的有效边界，必须对排除池进行抽样。作者推导了匹配的有限语料下界，证明排除池审计在零遗漏场景下是极小化最优的，并开发了一套精确的有限样本工具包，使用二项和超几何反演而非渐近近似，支持遗漏量认证、召回率转换、嵌套候选生成器族认证以及压力测试认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际经验流程中，第一轮高召回阶段决定了哪些项目进入后续审查、标注或建模，而它遗漏的相关项目将对所有后续阶段造成不可逆的损失。因此，需要一种方法能够以有限样本的有效性来认证这个被遗漏的相关项目数量是否足够小，但现有研究对此问题的标签复杂度缺乏系统性的理论刻画。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明仅对候选集内部标注无法认证遗漏量的非平凡边界，必须在排除池中采样；然后推导出零遗漏场景下的有限语料下界，证明排除池审计的极小化最优性；最后开发基于二项和超几何反演的精确有限样本工具包，而非传统的渐近近似方法。

Result: 任何有效的审计，如果要在没有遗漏时以高概率认证遗漏少于m个相关项，即使允许自适应标注整个包含池，也必须检查约N0/m量级的排除池标签。排除池审计在零遗漏场景下是极小化速率最优的。基于此开发的工具包能够同时认证遗漏量、转换为召回率、认证嵌套候选生成器族，以及产生针对声明扰动机制的压力测试证书。

Conclusion: 所有保证都基于一个严格的纪律：候选生成器（或预指定的族）和审计规则必须在查看认证标签之前固定。这套方法可以与可观测的审查负担相结合，选择满足遗漏量目标的最小负担候选生成器。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [236] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出主-从原型规划系统(MAPS)，一种分层深度强化学习架构，通过中央主智能体生成全局协调嵌入(proto-plan)，由分散工作智能体结合局部观测执行车辆控制，在无信号交叉口实现无碰撞导航，显著降低平均旅行时间，并展现出从3智能体到5智能体的零样本泛化能力(94%成功率)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无信号交叉口的自动驾驶车辆协调对多智能体强化学习系统仍是关键挑战，现有方法在组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或智能体设计刚性等方面存在不足。

Method: 提出MAPS分层DRL架构：中央Master智能体生成紧凑连续嵌入(proto-plan)编码全局协调策略；分散Worker智能体将嵌入与局部观测融合执行车辆控制，实现策略意图与战术执行的解耦及各模块独立优化。

Result: 在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航并显著降低平均旅行时间，超越SOTA基线；3智能体训练的模型在5智能体零样本部署中达94%成功率。

Conclusion: 基于proto-plan的分层学习为多车辆协调提供了有前景的框架。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [237] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对百万级上下文场景下原生MTP草案头因全注意力机制导致成本线性增长的问题，提出Windowed-MTP方法：仅对草案注意力施加滑动窗口+注意力汇，将KV工作集约束为常数，丢弃约99%的KV条目，在不影响验证分布的前提下实现每解码步成本降低28%-44%，并可通过环形缓冲区回收未读取的草案KV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码中，MTP草案头在长上下文（百万token）下执行全注意力导致成本随上下文线性增长，抵消甚至逆转投机加速收益，且此问题在混合/线性注意力目标模型下更为严重。

Method: 对草案注意力部分应用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口加注意力汇（attention sink），仅限制草案的KV工作集为常数；保留目标模型的全注意力验证不变。该方法免训练、即插即用，且无损（目标模型决定最终输出）。

Result: 在Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B和Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B三个架构家族、1M上下文单GPU环境下，每解码步成本降低28%-44%；可回收草案未读取的KV占总数7.7%-11%；端到端延迟匹配改善，部分场景下接受率也有所提升。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP通过约束草案注意力窗口，有效解决长上下文下投机解码的草案成本瓶颈，保持无损验证的同时显著提升效率，且不增加训练开销。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [238] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零流差异（ZFD）的两样本检验方法ZF2ST，通过学习局部错位方向模式来检测分布差异，在保持统计校准的同时实现了强大的检验效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两样本检验在处理高维结构化数据（如图像）时面临挑战，现有方法要么统计校准性差，要么对结构化分布变化不敏感。

Method: 基于零流准则定义统计差异量ZFD，将见证函数学习与假设评估分离，分别采用基于回归和最大化效力的方法学习见证函数，构建两样本检验流程ZF2ST。

Result: 在合成数据和图像数据集上，ZF2ST对结构化分布变化表现出强检验效力，同时维持了良好校准的I类错误率。

Conclusion: ZF2ST通过分离见证学习与假设评估，允许灵活使用神经网络，在不牺牲统计有效性的前提下实现了对结构化分布变化的高效检测。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [239] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X³-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架，将文本大语言模型的推理能力迁移至音频-语言模型，通过学生基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹、教师提供词级指导，并构建三层对称语料库（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理），在多项基准上显著提升音频推理与思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型在听觉感知上取得了显著进展，但在深层逻辑推理方面仍落后于基于文本的大语言模型，主要原因是缺乏高质量的音频推理数据。

Method: 提出X³-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架：(1) 学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹；(2) 教师模型使用匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供词级指导；(3) 构建三层对称语料库：文本推理转语音、基于复杂声学场景的音频事件推理、涉及副语言线索的口语对话推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR基准上，X³-OPD显著提升了音频推理能力和思维链质量，同时在域偏移下较好地保留了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态蒸馏和分级语料库设计，成功将文本推理能力迁移至音频模态，弥补了音频语言模型在深层逻辑推理上的不足，且扩展到了非语言事件、韵律和对话上下文等文本无法完全恢复的推理场景。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [240] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EnsembleEGNN，一种通过对每个构象使用共享的等变图神经网络（EGNN）编码，再用集合注意力块池化的分子构象集成基础模型。在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集上，从头训练失败（R²=0.005），但预训练模型达到R²=0.477和Pearson r=0.699，优于仅序列的BERT基线；与BERT联合端到端训练后进一步提升至R²=0.538和Pearson r=0.737。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子性质预测通常仅使用单一代表性构象，但许多分子在溶液中以构象集合形式存在，这忽略了构象多样性与热力学信息，导致预测性能受限。

Method: EnsembleEGNN采用共享EGNN层独立编码每个构象，然后通过集合注意力块（Set Attention Block）将构象表示池化为单一嵌入。预训练使用CREMP环肽数据集，采用掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重构和成对距离重构的多任务自监督目标。

Result: 在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集上：从头训练R²=0.005（几乎失败）；预训练模型R²=0.477、Pearson r=0.699，优于序列BERT（R²=0.439, r=0.667）；与BERT联合端到端训练后R²=0.538、Pearson r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集合编码为单一热力学信息嵌入能够显著改善环肽性质预测性能，表明构象集成表示优于单一构象或仅序列方法。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS是一个面向时间序列解释的必要性感知框架，通过两阶段设计结合反事实干预来识别既充分又必要的关键子序列，在合成和真实数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法仅关注充分性，可能导致解释中包含支持预测但非必要的虚假子序列，缺乏对必要性的考量。

Method: 两阶段设计：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程和充分性导向的解释掩码；Stage II通过反事实干预获取必要性信号，利用时序门控机制细化解释，抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要的部分。

Result: 在合成和真实时间序列基准上，TimePNS更准确地识别决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合反事实必要性分析能够有效过滤充分但不必要的虚假子序列，产出更精确的时间序列解释。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [242] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows)和Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)，一种通过逐步增广状态空间维度来实现可变大小生成的概率流框架，适用于连续和离散模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法自然地处理输出大小可变的生成任务（如图生成、变长序列生成）。

Method: 定义扩展插值过程，逐渐增广状态空间维度并添加条件噪声；提出EFMs，将任意时间步之间的映射分解为扩展算子（增广新坐标/令牌）和传输映射（沿插值前推），两者组合实现联合扩展与去噪。

Result: 在连续和离散（包括离散单纯形）空间中建立了EFlows和EFMs框架，能处理变尺寸图生成和变长序列生成，并指出固定画布流是EFMs的特例。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为输出大小本身作为可学习可控自由度的场景提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Verifier-First Evaluation of Agentic LLMs for Infrastructure-as-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20478)
*Mohamed Jouini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对七种基于智能体的Terraform生成策略进行了验证器优先的实证评估，发现主动检索、迭代精炼和提示优化能显著提升IaC生成质量，而79%的OPA失败源于信息缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言生成基础设施即代码(IaC)不仅需要语法正确的配置，还必须满足provider schema、依赖规划和组织策略约束。现有方法缺乏对这种验证约束的系统性研究，因此需要深入分析不同智能体策略在可验证IaC生成中的表现。

Method: 在IaC-Eval v2基准（186个AWS/Terraform任务，Rego v1策略）上评估七种智能体策略，将失败分为三种验证阶段（terraform validate、terraform plan、opa eval），并使用McNemar检验和Wilson置信区间进行成对比较（n=186, alpha=0.05）。

Result: (1) ReAct+MCP/ChromaDB RAG使Qwen2.5-Coder 7B的pass@1从14.0%提升至45.7%；(2)迭代精炼达到62.9%(Qwen 7B)和84.4%(GPT-4o) pass@1，呈现二元收敛；(3)GEPA反射优化在基线基础上提升+7.5 pp；(4)SIMBA无教师示范注入达到与Active RAG相当的性能，但无法解决SELF_DEFINED_PROPERTY错误；(5)79%的OPA失败是信息缺失问题，可通过提供策略文本解决。

Conclusion: 验证器反馈驱动的迭代精炼是最有效的策略；主动检索和提示优化能显著提升性能；大多数OPA失败源于生成器无法获取策略信息，而非推理能力不足，这为将策略直接集成到生成流程中提供了动机。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) generation from natural language requires satisfying provider schemas, dependency planning, and organizational policy constraints, not merely producing syntactically plausible configurations. We present a verifier-first empirical study of seven agentic strategies for Terraform generation evaluated on IaC-Eval v2, a modernized 186-task AWS/Terraform benchmark with Rego v1 intent policies. Our evaluation separates failures into three verifier stages (terraform validate, terraform plan, opa eval) and applies McNemar's test with Wilson confidence intervals on all pairwise comparisons (n=186, alpha=0.05).
  We report five principal findings. (1) Active retrieval via ReAct agents with MCP or ChromaDB-backed RAG raises Qwen2.5-Coder 7B from 14.0% to 45.7% pass@1 (p<0.0001), primarily by reducing VALIDATE_FAIL from 144 to 66 tasks. (2) Iterative refinement with verifier feedback achieves 62.9% (Qwen 7B) and 84.4% (GPT-4o) pass@1, exhibiting binary convergence -- tasks either resolve in one retry or exhaust the budget. (3) GEPA reflective instruction optimization raises the Active RAG baseline by +7.5 pp (p=0.026) using only 80 verifier-guided rollouts, providing evidence that prompt optimizers can improve verifiable IaC generation without weight updates. (4) SIMBA teacher-free demonstration injection achieves performance equivalent to Active RAG (p=1.0) without retrieval infrastructure, but fails to address the dominant SELF_DEFINED_PROPERTY error class (50% of failures). (5) A diagnostic Rego-injection experiment shows that 79% of post-refinement OPA failures are information-gap failures resolvable when policy text is visible (p=0.016), motivating policy

</details>


### [244] [Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation: to Enable More Secure Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20933)
*Jesse Phillips,Tracy Hall,Paul Rayson,Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合任务特定Transformer模型和大型语言模型（LLM）的源代码摘要生成方法（Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation），通过将Transformer生成的摘要用于LLM的提示工程，在BLEU-4上提升7.8%，在另一指标上提升5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码摘要对安全软件开发生命周期（SSDLC）的维护阶段至关重要，但现有摘要常缺失、不完整或过时。任务特定Transformer模型生成的摘要虽在NLG指标上得分高，但指标奖励的是词汇重叠而非摘要质量；而LLM能捕捉语义生成高质量摘要，但其抽象性导致与开发者编写的摘要用词差异大，在NLG指标上得分低。因此需要结合两者优势。

Method: 将任务特定Transformer生成的摘要用于LLM的提示工程中，辅助LLM生成更好的源代码摘要。使用四种不同的提示对四个LLM进行实验，其中任务特定Transformer在提示中协助LLM。

Result: BLEU-4提升7.8%，另一指标提升5%。

Conclusion: 结合Transformer生成摘要与LLM提示工程的方法能够提升源代码摘要质量，有助于软件从业者维护安全系统。

Abstract: Neural Source Code Summarisation (NSCS) aims to generate natural language summaries of source code to improve developers' and maintainers' understanding of code. Source code summaries are vital during the maintenance phase of the Secure Software Development Lifecycle (SSDLC), as they improve maintainers' understanding of code and help reduce the number of bugs and vulnerabilities in a software system. However, summaries are often missing, incomplete, or outdated in many software systems. Solutions to this problem use small, task-specific Transformer models or code-aware Large Language Models (LLMs). Task-specific Transformer-generated summaries often score well across many natural language generation (NLG) metrics, but these metrics reward lexical overlap rather than summary quality. Conversely, the ability of LLMs to capture semantics and produce high-quality summaries presents an exciting solution to this problem. This is especially relevant given the increased availability of LLMs and improvements in workstation hardware in recent years, which mean that some LLMs can now be run on developers' workstations. However, because of their abstractive nature, LLM-generated code summaries often differ greatly from developer-written summaries in the words and phrases they use, resulting in low scores across NLG metrics. We show how combining these two methods, by using Transformer-generated summaries in prompt engineering, may enable LLMs to create better source code summaries and help software practitioners maintain secure systems. We prompt four LLMs using four different prompts, with a task-specific Transformer used to assist the LLMs within the prompts. We present "Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation", a method through which we observe an improvement of 7.8% in BLEU-4 and 5%.

</details>


### [245] [Maintenance Signals in AI-Assisted GitHub Repositories: Evidence from GenAI Adopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21079)
*Rikuto Tsuchida,Youmei Fan,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）可以降低代码生成的工作量，但可能将工作转移到文档编写、验证、调试和维护上。本研究分析了GitHub上622名公开采用GenAI的用户、179个带有AI辅助配置文件的仓库、179个匹配的传统仓库以及248个AI辅助仓库中的问题，发现AI辅助仓库包含更长的README文件，而传统仓库包含更多外部URL。GenAI相关的问题常涉及外部依赖（如API速率限制和依赖GenAI提供商API），表明AI辅助将维护成本转向验证生成内容、管理外部AI依赖和验证AI特定行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究GenAI在降低代码生成工作量的同时，是否会将工作负担转移到文档编写、验证、调试和维护等环节，从而观察GenAI采用者中可观测的维护成本信号。

Method: 分析GitHub上622名公开采用GenAI的用户、179个带有AI辅助配置文件的仓库、179个匹配的传统仓库以及248个AI辅助仓库中的问题，对比AI辅助仓库与传统仓库的差异。

Result: AI辅助仓库包含更长的README文件（更多标题和代码块），传统仓库包含更多外部URL。GenAI相关的问题常涉及外部依赖，如API速率限制和依赖GenAI提供商API。

Conclusion: AI辅助将维护成本转向验证生成内容、管理外部AI依赖和验证AI特定行为。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) can reduce code-generation effort, but it may shift work to documentation, validation, debugging, and maintenance. We study observable maintenance-cost signals among GenAI adopters on GitHub by analyzing 622 users who publicly signal adoption, 179 repositories with visible AI-assistance configuration files, 179 matched traditional repositories, and 248 issues created in AI-assisted repositories. AI-assisted repositories span diverse project types and contain longer README files with more headers and code blocks, while traditional repositories contain more external URLs. Issues concerning GenAI technology often involve external dependencies, such as API rate limits and reliance on GenAI provider APIs. These findings suggest that AI assistance shifts maintenance costs toward verifying generated content, managing external AI dependencies, and validating AI-specific behavior.

</details>


### [246] [Improving Communication of Changes in Model-Based Engineering with Model-Independent Change Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21084)
*Philip Ochs,Lars Gesmann,Tobias Pett,Ina Schaefer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将形式化指定的变更（delta建模）映射为模型无关的自然语言描述，以促进跨学科团队在模型驱动工程中的沟通。通过混合方法评估，证明了技术可行性、适用性及可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型驱动工程中，跨学科团队通过模型协作开发系统，模型会随时间变化。但某一学科的工程师所做的变更难以被其他学科理解，现有方案中非正式语言缺乏客观性和标准化，而形式化变更缺乏人类可读性，无法支持跨学科沟通。

Method: 提出将delta建模中形式化指定的变更映射为模型无关语言描述的转换函数。采用混合方法评估：定量评估技术可行性（实现框架）和技术适用性（案例研究）；定性评估合理性、实际适用性和可扩展性（用户研究）。

Result: 实现了从形式化变更（delta建模）到模型无关自然语言描述的自动映射；定量评估验证了技术可行性与适用性；定性评估验证了合理性、实际适用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作是模型驱动工程中自动化、模型无关的变更描述的有前景的起点，有望弥合形式化变更理论与实际跨学科沟通需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: In model-based engineering, inter-disciplinary teams collaborate through models, which change over time for purposes of system development, what makes the proper description of such changes crucial for engineers. However, any change made by the engineer of one discipline will be difficult to understand by the engineers of other disciplines. To overcome this limitation, model-independent change descriptions can be derived instead, which preserve semantics of the changes and do not require model-specific knowledge. The two opposing approaches here are to describe changes using either informal language or formal notions of change. While informal language lacks objectivity and standardisation, formal notions of change lack human interpretability, and thus offering no support for inter-disciplinary communication. In this paper, we propose functions to map formally specified changes, represented in the approach of delta modelling, to change descriptions in model-independent language. In an exhaustive mixed-methods evaluation, we bridge the gap between the theoretical and the practical representation of changes. We quantitatively assess technical feasibility, with an implementation framework, and technical applicability, along a case study; and we qualitatively assess plausibility, practical applicability, and extensibility, in a user study. Our work shows a promising starting point for automated, model-independent description of changes in model-based engineering projects.

</details>


### [247] [Information is all you need: Requirements Engineering Quality Reframed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21319)
*Henning Femmer,Julian Frattini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的需求工程质量整体理论，将信息粒子（领域知识的离散片段）在角色和工件之间的传递建模为信息流，通过模拟展示了如何优化需求工程过程以改善信息传递效率，并为理解需求工程成功与失败提供了统一的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求工程质量研究过于模糊地诉诸"上下文"概念，缺乏系统性的理论来统一理解工件视角和过程视角下的需求工程质量。

Method: 提出信息粒子（information particles）概念，将需求工程过程建模为领域知识从信息源（如利益相关者）到信息目标（如开发人员、测试人员）的信息流，并结合工件与过程视角构建整体性理论。通过模拟实验对理论进行验证。

Result: 模拟展示了为什么高质量规格说明书在敏捷上下文中会被绕过，说明该模拟能够提供可操作的见解来校准需求工程过程以优化信息流。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅适用于组织层面的应用，还可以作为理解需求工程过程和工件成功或失败的统一理论框架。

Abstract: To move beyond this vague appeal to context, this vision proposes a novel holistic theory of requirements engineering (RE) quality. This theory models how information particles, i.e., discrete pieces of domain knowledge, are transferred between roles and artifacts. Since the RE process is ultimately an information transfer, holistic RE quality depends on the properties of information flow, i.e., how effectively and efficiently information is transferred from sources (like stakeholders) to targets (like developers and testers), uniting both artifact- and process-based perspectives on RE quality. In an exemplary simulation of the theory we illustrate why a high-quality specification gets bypassed in an agile context, thereby demonstrating that a simulation can provide actionable insights into calibrating the RE process to optimize the information flow. Beyond organizational applications, we envision that the theory can serve as a coherent theoretical framework for understanding the success or failure of RE processes and artifacts.

</details>


### [248] [Teaching Business Process Modeling to Leverage Soft Skills of Computing Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21344)
*Maria Istela Cagnin,Rick Kazman,Elisa Yumi Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过行业驱动的项目式教学方法，对53名计算机专业学生进行业务流程建模教学，发现该方法能有效提升学生的专业素养、主动性、动机、沟通能力和团队合作等软技能，为AI辅助软件开发时代的人才培养提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织高度依赖软件系统实现业务流程和战略目标，而AI虽提升了开发效率，但竞争激烈的就业市场要求计算机专业人员具备更强的软技能以获得竞争优势。

Method: 采用行业驱动的项目式教学方法，53名计算机学生与利益相关者互动并进行业务流程建模，系统收集学生反馈以评估所实践的软技能。

Result: 研究表明，专业素养、主动性、动机、沟通能力和团队合作等软技能可以通过业务流程建模教学得到提升。

Conclusion: 为计算机科学教育研究社区提供了证据，证明业务流程建模教学对未来专业人员特别有益，有助于在AI辅助软件开发时代培养其软技能。

Abstract: Background and Context: Organizations have highly depended on software systems to realize their business processes and achieve strategic business goals and competitive advantage; hence, computing professionals who can understand these processes could more effectively build such systems. At the same time, while Artificial Intelligence (AI) has increased the productivity in developing these and other systems, professionals should be better prepared due to the competitiveness of the job market; thus, soft skills could be an important factor to ensure their position in this market. Objective: This paper aims to analyze and present the impact of teaching business process modeling to leverage key soft skills of future computing professionals. Method and Findings: Our method involved an industry-driven, project-based approach with 53 computing students who actively interacted with stakeholders and modeled the organization's business process.
