We systematically collected the students' feedback, including the soft skills practiced and demonstrating that professionalism, initiative, motivation, communication, and teamwork can be leveraged. Implications: The main implication of this work for the Computer Science Education research community is to provide evidence that teaching business process modeling subjects is particularly beneficial for future professionals, as it could contribute to preparing them regarding their soft skills for the era of AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [249] [Toward Federated Cognitive Digital Twins over the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21357)
*Alessandra Somma,Alessio Bucaioni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦认知数字孪生（FCDT）架构，通过将联邦化与认知能力统一集成，在边缘到云的连续体中分布智能，以解决传统数字孪生架构在分布式系统中存在的可扩展性、延迟、弹性及语义推理不足等问题。局部孪生负责实时监控与轻量级认知，全局孪生执行系统级推理、仿真与协调，从而提升复杂分布式信息物理系统的可扩展性、响应性和决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生架构多采用集中式和单体设计，在智慧城市等分布式环境中面临可扩展性、延迟和弹性问题；同时，语义集成和高层推理能力有限，降低了基于孪生的决策有效性。联邦数字孪生虽通过分解系统提升了可扩展性，但智能仍集中在云端；认知数字孪生增强了语义推理和AI决策支持，但难以集成到分布式架构中。

Method: 提出联邦认知数字孪生架构，将联邦化与认知能力融合在统一框架中。架构沿边缘-云连续体分布智能：局部孪生部署在边缘端，提供实时监控和轻量级认知能力；全局孪生部署在云端，执行系统级推理、仿真和协调。通过分布式自治与认知推理的集成实现系统优化。

Result: 该方法在复杂分布式信息物理系统中提升了可扩展性、响应性和决策能力。

Conclusion: FCDT架构通过联邦化与认知能力的统一集成，有效克服了传统DT、FDT和CDT各自的局限性，为分布式CPS提供了一种兼顾实时性与全局智能的解决方案。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are increasingly adopted to monitor, analyze, and optimize Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) through continuous interaction between physical assets and their digital counterparts. However, current DT architectures often rely on centralized and monolithic designs, leading to scalability, latency, and resilience issues in distributed environment such as smart cities. Moreover, they provide limited support for semantic integration and high-level reasoning, reducing the effectiveness of DT-based decision-making. Recent studies on Federated Digital Twins (FDTs) have addressed scalability by decomposing complex systems into interacting twins, but they still largely centralize intelligence in cloud components. In parallel, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) enhance DTs with semantic reasoning, explainability, and AI-driven decision support, yet they are typically difficult to integrate into distributed architectures. This paper proposes a Federated Cognitive Digital Twin (FCDT) architecture that combines federation and cognition within a unified approach. The architecture distributes intelligence across the edge-to-cloud continuum through local twins, which provide real-time monitoring and lightweight cognitive capabilities, and global twins, which perform system-level reasoning, simulation, and coordination. By integrating distributed autonomy with cognitive reasoning, the proposed approach improves scalability, responsiveness, and decision-making in complex distributed CPSs

</details>


### [250] [From Resource Flow to Executable Tests: Petri-Net-Guided LLM Test Generation for Concurrent Stateful Rust APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21530)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Petri网引导的测试生成方法，用于并发有状态Rust API。该方法通过有色Petri网建模API资源、生命周期和因果依赖，生成合法深状态、近合法和偏序并发场景，作为LLM代码合成的约束中间表示。通过局部保真契约和结构修复循环保持建模意图，Petri引导的调度塑造优先处理高冲突并发骨架，最后用分层语义预言区分合成失败与API行为违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可以合成可执行的Rust测试，但输出经常违反API前置条件、内容浅显，或者将并发降级为偶然的顺序迹。而基于模型和系统化测试技术虽然能提供语义控制，但通常需要大量手写代码将抽象场景转化为可执行测试。本文旨在弥合形式化场景设计与低成本测试具体化之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 使用有色Petri网表示API资源、生命周期条件和因果依赖（彩色令牌和变迁）；2) 从Petri网推导合法深状态、近合法和偏序并发场景；3) 将这些场景作为LLM代码合成的约束中间表示；4) 通过局部保真契约和结构修复循环在具体化过程中保持建模意图；5) Petri引导的调度塑造优先处理高冲突并发骨架进行系统化探索；6) 使用分层语义预言区分合成失败与目标API预期行为违规。

Result: 该方法能够从Petri网模型系统化地生成多样化的并发测试场景，并通过约束中间表示和修复机制提升LLM代码合成的质量与保真度，最终有效区分合成过程中的失败与API行为本身的违规。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Petri网引导方法有效弥合了形式化并发场景设计与低成本测试具体化之间的鸿沟，为并发有状态Rust API的自动化测试生成提供了一种结合形式化建模与LLM代码合成的可行路径。

Abstract: Concurrent stateful library APIs expose behavior through evolving resource ownership, lifecycle states, and competing interleavings. Large language models can synthesize executable Rust tests, but their outputs often violate API preconditions, remain shallow, or reduce concurrency to accidental sequential traces. Conversely, model-based and systematic testing techniques provide semantic control but commonly require substantial handwritten code to turn abstract scenarios into executable tests. This paper addresses the gap between formal scenario design and low-cost test concretization. We present a Petri-net-guided methodology for test generation over concurrent stateful Rust APIs. The method represents API resources, lifecycle conditions, and causal dependencies as colored tokens and transitions; derives legal deep-state, near-legal, and partial-order concurrent scenarios; and uses these scenarios as a constrained intermediate representation for LLM-based code synthesis. A local-faithfulness contract and structural repair loop preserve the modeled intent during concretization, while Petri-guided schedule shaping prioritizes high-conflict concurrency skeletons for systematic exploration. A layered semantic oracle then distinguishes synthesis failures from violations of the target API's expected behavior.

</details>
